# Exit plan change log

Every app change the move-off-Supabase plan depends on is announced here by `EXIT_SYNC` (or the session that made it) with
`pnpm exit:map --acknowledge --by "<session>"`. Each notice lists what changed and which agents must check it against the
[exit infrastructure map](../engineering/exit/EXIT_INFRASTRUCTURE_MAP.md). The named agents record their review result under the
notice in their next task (`reviewed by <CALL> <date>: ok / follow-up <task>`). Protocol: [REDUNDANCY_TEAM](../engineering/exit/REDUNDANCY_TEAM.md) §4.

An announcement is a notice, not a review or an approval. Do not edit tool-written notices except to append review results.

- reviewed by `STORAGE` and `EXIT_SERVICES` functions 2026-10-21 (executor: Claude Code root session, not the pinned agents): the three variable-bucket callsites are bounded. `MediaUploadService.uploadToBucket` (`storage.upload` / `publicUrl`) is only ever called with literal lists — `[reels, game_highlights]` (Highlights), `[reels]` (story photos), `[avatars]` (avatars). `lib/media.ts publicMediaUrl` also receives buckets parsed from stored `sb:` references, but it refuses anything outside `PUBLIC_BUCKETS` = avatars / reels / game_highlights before calling the storage host, so no stored reference can mint a public URL for `reel_vault`, `fan_zone` or `beyond_game`; `lib/__tests__/media.test.ts` covers those refusals. An exit host must serve exactly these three public buckets plus the signed `reel_vault`. No code change.
